```ts
  afterAll(async () => { await ctx.app.close(); });
  beforeEach(async () => { await resetDb(ctx.dataSource); });
  const http = () => request(ctx.app.getHttpServer());

  it('signs up as client and reads /me', async () => {
    const { agent, email } = await signUp(ctx.app, { name: 'Martina Suárez' });
    const me = await agent.get('/api/me').expect(200);
    expect(me.body).toMatchObject({ name: 'Martina Suárez', email, role: 'client', phone: null, rehabFollowupEnabled: false });
  });

  it('cannot choose role on sign-up', async () => {
    const res = await http().post('/api/auth/sign-up/email').set('Origin', TEST_ORIGIN)
      .send({ name: 'X', email: 'x@test.move', password: 'Secreto123!', role: 'staff' });
    const [row] = await ctx.dataSource.query(`SELECT role FROM users WHERE email = 'x@test.move'`);
    expect(res.status === 200 ? row.role : 'client').toBe('client');
  });

  it('rejects anonymous /me with the error envelope', async () => {
    const res = await http().get('/api/me').expect(401);
    expect(res.body).toEqual({ statusCode: 401, code: 'UNAUTHENTICATED', message: 'Tenés que iniciar sesión.' });
  });

  it('keeps one account per email (case-insensitive)', async () => {
    await signUp(ctx.app, { email: 'Ana@Test.move' });
    await http().post('/api/auth/sign-up/email').set('Origin', TEST_ORIGIN)
      .send({ name: 'Ana 2', email: 'ana@test.move', password: 'Secreto123!' });
    const [{ count }] = await ctx.dataSource.query(`SELECT count(*)::int AS count FROM users WHERE LOWER(email) = 'ana@test.move'`);
    expect(count).toBe(1);
  });

  it('signs out', async () => {
    const { agent } = await signUp(ctx.app);
    await agent.post('/api/auth/sign-out').set('Origin', TEST_ORIGIN).expect(200);
    await agent.get('/api/me').expect(401);
  });

  it('updates the profile', async () => {
    const { agent } = await signUp(ctx.app);
    const res = await agent.patch('/api/me').send({ phone: '099123456' }).expect(200);
    expect(res.body.phone).toBe('099123456');
    await agent.patch('/api/me').send({ role: 'staff' }).expect(400);
  });

  it('invites a user who then sets a password through the reset flow', async () => {
    const { userId } = await ctx.app.get(AuthService).createInvitedUser({ name: 'Lucía Pereira', email: 'lu@test.move' });
    const [log] = await ctx.dataSource.query(`SELECT template, user_id, idempotency_key FROM email_logs`);
    expect(log.template).toBe('account-invitation');
    const [verification] = await ctx.dataSource.query(`SELECT identifier FROM verifications ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1`);
    const token = String(verification.identifier).replace(/^reset-password:/, '');
    expect(log.idempotency_key).not.toContain(token);
    await http().post('/api/auth/reset-password').set('Origin', TEST_ORIGIN).send({ newPassword: 'NuevaClave123!', token }).expect(200);
    const login = request.agent(ctx.app.getHttpServer());
    await login.post('/api/auth/sign-in/email').set('Origin', TEST_ORIGIN).send({ email: 'lu@test.move', password: 'NuevaClave123!' }).expect(200);
    expect((await login.get('/api/me').expect(200)).body.id).toBe(userId);
  });

  it('rejects inviting an existing email', async () => {
    await signUp(ctx.app, { email: 'dup@test.move' });
    await expect(ctx.app.get(AuthService).createInvitedUser({ name: 'D', email: 'DUP@test.move' }))
      .rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'EMAIL_ALREADY_REGISTERED' });
  });
});
```

Si el formato del `identifier` de la verificación difiere en la versión instalada, ajustar la extracción del token y anotarlo en el reporte.

### Task T-006: Layouts, navegación y guards de rutas en la web
**Model:** sonnet · **Depends on:** T-003 integrated · **Parallel with:** T-005, T-007, T-008 · **Contracts:** HTTP contract, Frontend route map · **Vault:** systems/Panel staff web, systems/Area cliente web, concepts/Sistema visual MOVE

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/src/app/(public)/layout.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/(cliente)/layout.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/staff/layout.tsx`
- Create: `apps/web/src/components/layout/public-header.tsx`, `public-footer.tsx`, `client-header.tsx`, `staff-sidebar.tsx`, `staff-topbar.tsx`, `logout-button.tsx`, `notices-bell.tsx`, `staff-sidebar.test.tsx`, `public-header.test.tsx` (todos en `apps/web/src/components/layout/`)
- Create: `apps/web/src/lib/auth/session.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/auth/session.test.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/auth/safe-next.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/auth/safe-next.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `serverApiFetchOrNull`, `apiFetch`, `authClient`, tipos (`MeDto`, `NoticeDto`, `AlertCountsDto`), `PageContainer`, `initials` (T-003).
- Produces:
  - `getCurrentUser(): Promise<MeDto | null>` (server; `GET /me`, `null` si 401).
  - `requireRole(role: Role, currentPath: string): Promise<MeDto>` (server; redirige con `redirect()` de `next/navigation` según los guards del route map).
  - `safeNextPath(next: string | null | undefined, role: Role): string | null` (solo rutas internas que empiezan con `/` y no con `//`; `/staff*` solo para staff; rutas cliente solo para client).
  - Layouts de los grupos `(public)`, `(cliente)` y `staff` con la navegación exacta del route map. `StaffSidebar` muestra el badge de alertas (`GET /staff/alerts/counts`, React Query, refetch cada 60 s) y marca el ítem activo por `usePathname()`.
  - `LogoutButton` (`authClient.signOut()` y `router.replace('/')`).

**Acceptance criteria:**
1. El layout público muestra navbar sticky (`h-16`, `bg-background/90` con blur), links Inicio/Performance/Rehab/Recovery/Planes, "Ingresar" (o "Mi perfil"/"Panel" según rol), "Reservar", y footer con "© 2026 MOVE Strength & Conditioning", como `01-sitio-publico.html`.
2. `(cliente)` exige sesión de cliente; `staff` exige sesión de staff; los redirects siguen el route map, con `next` = ruta actual.
3. La sidebar staff tiene los 6 ítems (con subitem Check-in), el ítem activo resaltado y el badge con el total de alertas abiertas (oculto si es 0).
4. `safeNextPath` rechaza URLs externas (`https://…`, `//evil.com`) y rutas de otro rol.
5. Lo que lee cookies está dentro de `<Suspense>` (cacheComponents).

Cómo obtener la ruta actual en un layout de servidor: si no hay API directa, cada layout pasa la ruta del grupo y el componente cliente usa `usePathname()`; documentar la elección en el reporte.

```ts
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';

const redirect = vi.fn((url: string) => { throw new Error(`REDIRECT:${url}`); });
vi.mock('next/navigation', () => ({ redirect }));
const serverApiFetchOrNull = vi.fn();
vi.mock('@/lib/api/server', () => ({ serverApiFetchOrNull }));

const { requireRole } = await import('./session');

describe('requireRole', () => {
  beforeEach(() => { redirect.mockClear(); serverApiFetchOrNull.mockReset(); });

  it('sends anonymous users to login with next', async () => {
    serverApiFetchOrNull.mockResolvedValue(null);
    await expect(requireRole('client', '/perfil')).rejects.toThrow('REDIRECT:/ingresar?next=%2Fperfil');
  });

  it('sends staff away from the client area and clients away from staff', async () => {
    serverApiFetchOrNull.mockResolvedValue({ id: '1', role: 'staff' });
    await expect(requireRole('client', '/perfil')).rejects.toThrow('REDIRECT:/staff');
    serverApiFetchOrNull.mockResolvedValue({ id: '2', role: 'client' });
    await expect(requireRole('staff', '/staff')).rejects.toThrow('REDIRECT:/perfil');
  });

  it('returns the user when the role matches', async () => {
    serverApiFetchOrNull.mockResolvedValue({ id: '2', role: 'client' });
    await expect(requireRole('client', '/perfil')).resolves.toMatchObject({ id: '2' });
  });
});
```

### Task T-008: Páginas de acceso (ingresar, registro, recuperar, restablecer, continuar)
**Model:** sonnet · **Depends on:** T-003 integrated · **Parallel with:** T-005, T-006, T-007 · **Contracts:** HTTP contract, Frontend route map · **Vault:** screens/Pantalla acceso, constraints/Cuenta se crea durante la reserva

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/src/app/(auth)/layout.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/(auth)/ingresar/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/(auth)/registro/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/(auth)/recuperar/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/(auth)/restablecer/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/(auth)/continuar/page.tsx`
- Create: `apps/web/src/components/auth/login-form.tsx`, `register-form.tsx`, `forgot-password-form.tsx`, `reset-password-form.tsx`, `google-button.tsx`, `auth-tabs.tsx`, `profiles-info.tsx`, `schemas.ts`, `login-form.test.tsx`, `register-form.test.tsx`, `reset-password-form.test.tsx` (todos en `apps/web/src/components/auth/`)
- Create: `apps/web/src/lib/auth/post-login.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/auth/post-login.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `authClient`, `apiFetch`, `MeDto`, `PageContainer`, `DisplayTitle`, `Eyebrow` (T-003); componentes shadcn `input`, `label`, `button`, `tabs`.
- Produces:
  - `schemas.ts`: `loginSchema` (email, password ≥1), `registerSchema` (name 2–80, email, password ≥8, phone opcional 6–20), `forgotSchema` (email), `resetSchema` (password ≥8, confirm igual).
  - `resolvePostLogin(me: MeDto, next: string | null): string` — misma regla que `safeNextPath` de T-006, reimplementada acá (T-006 es de la misma ola): `next` interno y permitido para el rol, o `/staff` / `/perfil`.
  - Ingreso exitoso (email o Google) → `/continuar?next=<next>`; `/continuar` (cliente) llama `GET /me` y hace `router.replace(resolvePostLogin(me, next))`.
  - Google: botón visible solo si `process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_GOOGLE_ENABLED === 'true'`; `authClient.signIn.social({ provider: 'google', callbackURL: `${window.location.origin}/continuar?next=…` })`.
  - Recuperar: `authClient.requestPasswordReset({ email, redirectTo: `${window.location.origin}/restablecer` })` (o el nombre instalado) y siempre el mismo mensaje: "Si el email está registrado, te enviamos un link para restablecer tu contraseña."
  - Restablecer: lee `?token=`; `authClient.resetPassword({ newPassword, token })`; éxito → "Listo, ya podés ingresar" con link a `/ingresar`; sin token o token inválido → mensaje y link a `/recuperar`.

**Acceptance criteria:**
1. `/ingresar` reproduce `02-perfiles-usuario.html`: navbar mínima "← Volver al sitio", "Entrá a MOVE.", tabs "Iniciar sesión" / "Crear cuenta" (esta última navega a `/registro` conservando `next`), card de login, "¿Olvidaste tu contraseña?", divisor "o continuá con", Google (si está habilitado), nota "¿Primera vez en MOVE? Podés reservar Rehab o Recovery sin ser alumno — tu cuenta se crea durante el proceso." y las 3 cards de perfiles.
2. Los formularios validan con Zod antes de enviar y muestran errores por campo en español.
3. Credenciales inválidas muestran "Email o contraseña incorrectos." sin revelar si el email existe; email ya registrado en `/registro` muestra "Ya existe una cuenta con ese email." con link a ingresar.
4. Después de ingresar o registrarse, el usuario llega a su área según rol, o a `next` si es válido para su rol.

Revisar en `apps/web/node_modules/better-auth/` los métodos del cliente de React (`signIn.email`, `signUp.email`, `signIn.social`, `signOut`, `requestPasswordReset`/`forgetPassword`, `resetPassword`) y la forma de sus errores (`{ data, error }`). Usar los nombres instalados.

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { MeDto } from '@/lib/api/types';
import { resolvePostLogin } from './post-login';

const me = (role: MeDto['role']): MeDto => ({ id: '1', name: 'A', email: 'a@x', role, phone: null, rehabFollowupEnabled: false });

describe('resolvePostLogin', () => {
  it('honors a valid next for the role', () => {
    expect(resolvePostLogin(me('client'), '/performance')).toBe('/performance');
    expect(resolvePostLogin(me('staff'), '/staff/turnos')).toBe('/staff/turnos');
  });
  it('falls back to the role home', () => {
    expect(resolvePostLogin(me('client'), '/staff')).toBe('/perfil');
    expect(resolvePostLogin(me('staff'), null)).toBe('/staff');
    expect(resolvePostLogin(me('client'), 'https://evil.com')).toBe('/perfil');
  });
});
```

```tsx
import { render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';

const push = vi.fn();
vi.mock('next/navigation', () => ({ useRouter: () => ({ push, replace: push }), useSearchParams: () => new URLSearchParams('next=/performance') }));
const signInEmail = vi.fn();
vi.mock('@/lib/auth-client', () => ({ authClient: { signIn: { email: signInEmail } } }));

const { LoginForm } = await import('./login-form');

describe('LoginForm', () => {
  beforeEach(() => { push.mockReset(); signInEmail.mockReset(); });

  it('validates before submitting', async () => {
    render(<LoginForm />);
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Ingresar' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText('Ingresá un email válido.')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(signInEmail).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('signs in and continues with next', async () => {
    signInEmail.mockResolvedValue({ data: { user: { id: '1' } }, error: null });
    render(<LoginForm />);
    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText('Email'), 'ana@test.move');
    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText('Contraseña'), 'Secreto123!');
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Ingresar' }));
    expect(signInEmail).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ email: 'ana@test.move', password: 'Secreto123!' });
    expect(push).toHaveBeenCalledWith('/continuar?next=%2Fperformance');
  });

  it('shows a generic error on bad credentials', async () => {
    signInEmail.mockResolvedValue({ data: null, error: { status: 401, message: 'Invalid email or password' } });
    render(<LoginForm />);
    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText('Email'), 'ana@test.move');
    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText('Contraseña'), 'mala');
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Ingresar' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText('Email o contraseña incorrectos.')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});
```

**Acceptance criteria:**
1. `GET /api/catalog/plans?service=performance` devuelve solo planes activos y no borrados de ese servicio, ordenados por `sortOrder`, con la forma `PlanDto`; sin sesión.
2. Staff crea, edita (sin cambiar `service` ni `kind`), desactiva y borra (soft) planes; `slug` duplicado → 409 `SLUG_TAKEN`; combinación inválida → 400 `PLAN_KIND_INVALID`.
3. Un cliente en `/api/staff/plans` recibe 403 `FORBIDDEN`; sin sesión, 401.
4. `GET /api/settings/public` devuelve `{ cancelLimitHours, bookingWindowDays }`; `PATCH /api/staff/settings` valida rangos (DTO) y la regla cruzada (`SETTINGS_INVALID`), y registra `updated_by`.

```ts
import { validatePlanShape } from './plan-rules.js';

const base = { periodMonths: null, periodDays: null, sessionsIncluded: null, durationMinutes: null };

describe('validatePlanShape', () => {
  it('accepts the four valid shapes', () => {
    expect(() => validatePlanShape({ ...base, service: 'performance', kind: 'period_plan', periodMonths: 3 })).not.toThrow();
    expect(() => validatePlanShape({ ...base, service: 'recovery', kind: 'session_pack', periodDays: 30, sessionsIncluded: 4, durationMinutes: 45 })).not.toThrow();
    expect(() => validatePlanShape({ ...base, service: 'recovery', kind: 'single_session', durationMinutes: 45 })).not.toThrow();
    expect(() => validatePlanShape({ ...base, service: 'rehab', kind: 'evaluation', durationMinutes: 60 })).not.toThrow();
  });

    expect(() => validatePlanShape({ ...base, service: 'performance', kind: 'period_plan' })).toThrow();
    expect(() => validatePlanShape({ ...base, service: 'recovery', kind: 'session_pack', periodDays: 30 })).toThrow();
```

```ts
import request from 'supertest';
import { seedCatalogAndSchedule } from '../src/database/seed-data.js';
import { createTestApp, type TestApp } from './utils/app.js';
import { signUpClient, signUpStaff } from './utils/auth.js';
import { resetDb } from './utils/db.js';

describe('catalog (e2e)', () => {
  let ctx: TestApp;
  beforeAll(async () => { ctx = await createTestApp(); });
  afterAll(async () => { await ctx.app.close(); });
  beforeEach(async () => { await resetDb(ctx.dataSource); await seedCatalogAndSchedule(ctx.dataSource); });

  it('lists active plans publicly in order', async () => {
    const res = await request(ctx.app.getHttpServer()).get('/api/catalog/plans?service=performance').expect(200);
    expect(res.body.map((p: { slug: string }) => p.slug)).toEqual(['performance-mensual', 'performance-trimestral', 'performance-semestral']);
    expect(res.body[1]).toMatchObject({ price: 10500, currency: 'UYU', periodMonths: 3, featured: true });
  });

  it('lets staff manage plans and hides inactive ones from the public list', async () => {
    const { agent } = await signUpStaff(ctx.app, ctx.dataSource);
    const created = await agent.post('/api/staff/plans')
      .send({ service: 'performance', kind: 'period_plan', slug: 'performance-anual', name: 'Plan Anual', price: 36000, periodMonths: 12 })
      .expect(201);
    await agent.post('/api/staff/plans')
      .send({ service: 'performance', kind: 'period_plan', slug: 'performance-anual', name: 'Dup', price: 1, periodMonths: 1 })
      .expect(409).expect((r) => expect(r.body.code).toBe('SLUG_TAKEN'));
    await agent.patch(`/api/staff/plans/${created.body.id}`).send({ active: false }).expect(200);
    const pub = await request(ctx.app.getHttpServer()).get('/api/catalog/plans?service=performance').expect(200);
    expect(pub.body.some((p: { slug: string }) => p.slug === 'performance-anual')).toBe(false);
    await agent.delete(`/api/staff/plans/${created.body.id}`).expect(204);
  });

  it('rejects invalid shapes and non-staff users', async () => {
    const staff = await signUpStaff(ctx.app, ctx.dataSource);
    await staff.agent.post('/api/staff/plans')
      .send({ service: 'recovery', kind: 'period_plan', slug: 'bad', name: 'Bad', price: 1, periodMonths: 1 })
      .expect(400).expect((r) => expect(r.body.code).toBe('PLAN_KIND_INVALID'));
    const client = await signUpClient(ctx.app);
    await client.agent.get('/api/staff/plans').expect(403);
    await request(ctx.app.getHttpServer()).get('/api/staff/plans').expect(401);
  });
});
```

**Model:** sonnet · **Depends on:** T-005 integrated · **Parallel with:** T-009, T-011, T-012 · **Contracts:** Data contract, HTTP contract, Domain rules · **Vault:** systems/Modulo agenda, decisions/ADR calendario propio, concepts/Horario, concepts/Disponibilidad profesional, constraints/Horario Rehab se bloquea al reservar

- Create: `apps/api/src/modules/schedule/controllers/staff-professionals.controller.ts`, `staff-templates.controller.ts`, `staff-slots.controller.ts` (en `apps/api/src/modules/schedule/controllers/`)
- Create: `apps/api/src/modules/schedule/services/schedule.service.ts`, `apps/api/src/modules/schedule/services/professionals.service.ts`, `apps/api/src/modules/schedule/services/templates.service.ts`
- Create: `apps/api/src/modules/schedule/domain/slot-generation.ts`, `apps/api/src/modules/schedule/domain/slot-generation.spec.ts`
- Create: `apps/api/src/modules/schedule/jobs/generate-slots.job.ts`
- Create: `apps/api/src/modules/schedule/dto/*.ts` (create/update professional, create/update template, create slot, generate), `apps/api/src/modules/schedule/models/time-slot.model.ts`, `apps/api/src/modules/schedule/models/professional.model.ts`
- Create: `apps/api/test/schedule.e2e-spec.ts`
- Modify: `apps/api/src/app.module.ts` (registrar `ScheduleModule.forRoot()` de `@nestjs/schedule` —lo usan todos los crons del plan— y `AgendaModule`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `AppError`, `Roles`, `JobRunner`, helpers de R1 (T-001); entidades `Professional`, `ScheduleTemplate`, `TimeSlot` (T-002); `SettingsService` (T-002).
- Produces:
  - `TimeSlotModel { id: string; service: Service; startsAt: Date; endsAt: Date; capacity: number; professional: { id: string; name: string } | null; templateId: string | null; cancelledAt: Date | null }`.
  - `ScheduleService` (clase en `services/schedule.service.ts`, exportada por `AgendaModule`):
    - `lockSlot(manager: EntityManager, slotId: string): Promise<TimeSlotModel>` — `SELECT … FOR UPDATE` sobre `time_slots`; lanza `SLOT_NOT_FOUND`.
    - `getSlot(slotId): Promise<TimeSlotModel>` (lanza `SLOT_NOT_FOUND`).
    - `listSlots(opts: { service?: Service; from: Date; to: Date; includeCancelled?: boolean }): Promise<TimeSlotModel[]>` (orden `startsAt`).
    - `cancelSlot(manager, slotId): Promise<TimeSlotModel>`; `updateCapacity(manager, slotId, capacity): Promise<TimeSlotModel>` (rehab: solo 1, si no `TEMPLATE_INVALID`).
    - `createSlot(input: { service; date; startTime; durationMinutes; capacity; professionalId? }): Promise<TimeSlotModel>`.
    - `generateSlots(fromDate: string, days: number): Promise<number>` (idempotente por `UQ_time_slots_template_start`).
  - `buildSlotsFromTemplates(templates: TemplateSpec[], fromDate: string, days: number): SlotSpec[]` (pura) con `TemplateSpec { id; service; weekday; startTime; durationMinutes; capacity; professionalId }` y `SlotSpec { templateId; service; startsAt: Date; endsAt: Date; capacity; professionalId }`.
  - Job `generate-slots`: cron `0 3 * * *` (`timeZone: BUSINESS_TZ`) y una vez en `onApplicationBootstrap`, ambos con `JobRunner.run('generate-slots', …)` y `days = bookingWindowDays + 1` desde `businessToday()`.
  - Endpoints de agenda staff del HTTP contract (owner T-010). `POST /staff/time-slots` responde `StaffTimeSlotDto` con contadores en 0.

**Acceptance criteria:**
1. Las plantillas generan un turno por fecha cuyo `isoWeekday` coincide, con `starts_at = businessDateTime(fecha, startTime)` y `ends_at = starts_at + durationMinutes`, para `[fromDate, fromDate + days)`.
2. Generar dos veces no duplica turnos; desactivar o borrar una plantilla detiene la generación futura sin tocar turnos ya generados; editar una plantilla afecta solo turnos que se generen después.
3. Plantillas y turnos de Rehab exigen profesional y cupo 1 (`TEMPLATE_INVALID`); profesional inexistente → `PROFESSIONAL_NOT_FOUND`.
4. Al crear o editar una plantilla se generan sus turnos dentro de la ventana de reserva.
5. Todos los endpoints son `staff` (403 para clientes).

```ts
import { buildSlotsFromTemplates } from './slot-generation.js';

const template = { id: 't1', service: 'performance' as const, weekday: 1, startTime: '19:00', durationMinutes: 60, capacity: 14, professionalId: null };

describe('buildSlotsFromTemplates', () => {
  it('creates slots on matching weekdays in Montevideo time', () => {

    expect(slots[0]).toMatchObject({ templateId: 't1', capacity: 14, service: 'performance' });
  });

  it('excludes the end date', () => {
    expect(buildSlotsFromTemplates([template], '2026-10-13', 6)).toHaveLength(0);
  });
});
```

```ts
import { addDays, businessToday } from '../src/common/time/business-time.js';
import { ScheduleService } from '../src/modules/schedule/services/schedule.service.js';
import { createTestApp, type TestApp } from './utils/app.js';
import { signUpClient, signUpStaff } from './utils/auth.js';
import { resetDb } from './utils/db.js';

describe('schedule (e2e)', () => {
  let ctx: TestApp;
  beforeAll(async () => { ctx = await createTestApp(); });
  afterAll(async () => { await ctx.app.close(); });
  beforeEach(async () => { await resetDb(ctx.dataSource); });

  it('creates templates, generates idempotently and validates rehab', async () => {
    const { agent } = await signUpStaff(ctx.app, ctx.dataSource);
    const pro = await agent.post('/api/staff/professionals').send({ name: 'Fisio Uno' }).expect(201);
    await agent.post('/api/staff/schedule/templates')
      .send({ service: 'rehab', weekday: 2, startTime: '11:00', durationMinutes: 60, capacity: 2, professionalId: pro.body.id })
      .expect(400).expect((r) => expect(r.body.code).toBe('TEMPLATE_INVALID'));
    for (let weekday = 1; weekday <= 7; weekday += 1) {
      await agent.post('/api/staff/schedule/templates')
        .send({ service: 'performance', weekday, startTime: '23:59', durationMinutes: 60, capacity: 14 }).expect(201);
    }
    const [{ count: first }] = await ctx.dataSource.query(`SELECT count(*)::int AS count FROM time_slots`);
    expect(first).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(14);
    const again = await agent.post('/api/staff/schedule/generate').send({}).expect(201);
    expect(again.body.created).toBe(0);
  });

  it('locks a slot row inside a transaction', async () => {
    const { agent } = await signUpStaff(ctx.app, ctx.dataSource);
    const slot = await agent.post('/api/staff/time-slots')
      .send({ service: 'performance', date: addDays(businessToday(), 1), startTime: '19:00', durationMinutes: 60, capacity: 14 }).expect(201);
    const schedule = ctx.app.get(ScheduleService);
    const locked = await ctx.dataSource.transaction((m) => schedule.lockSlot(m, slot.body.id));
    expect(locked).toMatchObject({ id: slot.body.id, capacity: 14 });
    await expect(ctx.dataSource.transaction((m) => schedule.lockSlot(m, '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000')))
      .rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'SLOT_NOT_FOUND' });
  });

  it('is staff only', async () => {
    const { agent } = await signUpClient(ctx.app);
    await agent.get('/api/staff/schedule/templates').expect(403);
  });
});
```

### Task T-011: Suscripciones — vigencia, renovación y paquetes Recovery
**Model:** opus · **Depends on:** T-005 integrated · **Parallel with:** T-009, T-010, T-012 · **Contracts:** Data contract, HTTP contract, Domain rules · **Vault:** systems/Modulo suscripciones, concepts/Suscripcion, constraints/Renovacion extiende desde el vencimiento, concepts/Paquete de sesiones Recovery, constraints/Sesiones Recovery no exceden el paquete, concepts/Sesiones Recovery no usadas, failures/Dias de plan perdidos al renovar

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/api/src/modules/subscriptions/subscriptions.module.ts`, `apps/api/src/modules/subscriptions/controllers/my-subscriptions.controller.ts`, `apps/api/src/modules/subscriptions/services/subscriptions.service.ts`
- Create: `apps/api/src/modules/subscriptions/domain/vigencia.ts`, `apps/api/src/modules/subscriptions/domain/vigencia.spec.ts`
- Create: `apps/api/src/modules/subscriptions/models/subscription.model.ts`
- Create: `apps/api/test/subscriptions.e2e-spec.ts`
- Modify: `apps/api/src/app.module.ts` (registrar `SubscriptionsModule`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: helpers R1, `AppError`, `Roles`, `CurrentUser` (T-001); entidades `Subscription`, `Plan` (T-002, la relación `plan` se usa para leer `name`); `SettingsService` (`expiringSoonDays`).
- Produces:
  - `SubscriptionModel { id; userId; planId; planName; service: 'performance' | 'recovery'; startsOn: string; endsOn: string; sessionsTotal: number | null; sessionsUsed: number; purchaseId: string | null; createdAt: Date }`.
  - `PlanSnapshot { id: string; name: string; service: Service; kind: PlanKind; periodMonths: number | null; periodDays: number | null; sessionsIncluded: number | null }`.
  - Funciones puras (`domain/vigencia.ts`): `computeActivationPeriod(plan: PlanSnapshot, latestPerformanceEndsOn: string | null, today: string): { startsOn: string; endsOn: string; sessionsTotal: number | null }` (R3); `covers(sub, date): boolean` (R2); `performanceSummary(subs: SubscriptionModel[], today: string): { current; upcoming; latest }` (R4); `toSubscriptionDto(sub, today, expiringSoonDays): SubscriptionDto` (R4); `toPackDto(sub): PackDto`.
  - `SubscriptionsService`:
    - `activateFromPurchase(manager: EntityManager, input: { purchaseId: string; userId: string; plan: PlanSnapshot; today: string }): Promise<SubscriptionModel>` (R3; idempotente por `purchase_id`; lanza `PLAN_KIND_INVALID` si el kind no es `period_plan` ni `session_pack`).
    - `findCoveringPerformance(manager: EntityManager, userId: string, date: string): Promise<SubscriptionModel | null>`.
    - `consumePackSession(manager: EntityManager, userId: string, date: string): Promise<SubscriptionModel | null>` (R5).
    - `releasePackSession(manager: EntityManager, subscriptionId: string): Promise<void>`.
    - `getMySubscriptions(userId: string, today: string): Promise<MySubscriptionsDto>`.
    - `listPerformanceByUsers(userIds: string[]): Promise<SubscriptionModel[]>` y `listActivePacksByUsers(userIds: string[], today: string): Promise<SubscriptionModel[]>`.
    - `findById(id): Promise<SubscriptionModel | null>`.
  - `GET /api/me/subscriptions` (client).

**Acceptance criteria:**
1. R3 exacto: compra inicial, renovación anticipada (sin perder días) y renovación después de vencer; paquete de 30 días con su saldo.
2. Activar dos veces la misma compra devuelve la misma suscripción (una sola fila).
3. `findCoveringPerformance` cubre el último día completo ("vence el 15/11" cubre el 15/11) y no el 16/11.
4. Dos consumos concurrentes sobre un paquete con 1 sesión dejan exactamente uno exitoso; con saldo 0 devuelve `null`; `releasePackSession` devuelve la sesión.
5. `GET /api/me/subscriptions` devuelve `MySubscriptionsDto` con `status`, `daysLeft` y `progressPct` según R4.

```ts
import { computeActivationPeriod, covers, toSubscriptionDto } from './vigencia.js';

const monthly = { id: 'p1', name: 'Plan Mensual', service: 'performance' as const, kind: 'period_plan' as const, periodMonths: 1, periodDays: null, sessionsIncluded: null };
const quarterly = { ...monthly, id: 'p3', name: 'Plan Trimestral', periodMonths: 3 };
const pack4 = { id: 'r4', name: 'Paquete 4 sesiones', service: 'recovery' as const, kind: 'session_pack' as const, periodMonths: null, periodDays: 30, sessionsIncluded: 4 };

describe('computeActivationPeriod', () => {
  it('starts today on first purchase', () => {
    expect(computeActivationPeriod(quarterly, null, '2026-08-15')).toEqual({ startsOn: '2026-08-15', endsOn: '2026-11-15', sessionsTotal: null });
  });
  it('extends from the current end when renewing early', () => {
    expect(computeActivationPeriod(monthly, '2026-11-15', '2026-11-10')).toEqual({ startsOn: '2026-11-16', endsOn: '2026-12-15', sessionsTotal: null });
  });
  it('restarts today after expiry', () => {
    expect(computeActivationPeriod(monthly, '2026-10-02', '2026-10-12')).toEqual({ startsOn: '2026-10-12', endsOn: '2026-11-12', sessionsTotal: null });
  });
  it('gives packs 30 days and their sessions', () => {
    expect(computeActivationPeriod(pack4, null, '2026-10-01')).toEqual({ startsOn: '2026-10-01', endsOn: '2026-10-30', sessionsTotal: 4 });
  });
});

describe('covers and DTO', () => {
  const sub = { id: 's', userId: 'u', planId: 'p3', planName: 'Plan Trimestral', service: 'performance' as const, startsOn: '2026-08-15', endsOn: '2026-11-15', sessionsTotal: null, sessionsUsed: 0, purchaseId: null, createdAt: new Date() };
  it('covers the whole last day', () => {
    expect(covers(sub, '2026-11-15')).toBe(true);
    expect(covers(sub, '2026-11-16')).toBe(false);
  });
  it('matches the profile mockup numbers', () => {
    expect(toSubscriptionDto(sub, '2026-10-12', 7)).toMatchObject({ status: 'active', daysLeft: 34, progressPct: 63 });
    expect(toSubscriptionDto(sub, '2026-11-10', 7).status).toBe('expiring');
  });
});
```

```ts
import { addDays, businessToday } from '../src/common/time/business-time.js';
import { seedCatalogAndSchedule } from '../src/database/seed-data.js';
import { SubscriptionsService } from '../src/modules/subscriptions/services/subscriptions.service.js';
import { createTestApp, type TestApp } from './utils/app.js';
import { signUpClient } from './utils/auth.js';
import { resetDb } from './utils/db.js';

describe('subscriptions (e2e)', () => {
  let ctx: TestApp;
  let subs: SubscriptionsService;
  beforeAll(async () => { ctx = await createTestApp(); subs = ctx.app.get(SubscriptionsService); });
  afterAll(async () => { await ctx.app.close(); });
  beforeEach(async () => { await resetDb(ctx.dataSource); await seedCatalogAndSchedule(ctx.dataSource); });

  const planBySlug = async (slug: string) => {
    const [row] = await ctx.dataSource.query(
      `SELECT id, name, service, kind, period_months AS "periodMonths", period_days AS "periodDays", sessions_included AS "sessionsIncluded" FROM plans WHERE slug = $1`, [slug]);
    return row;
  };
  const purchase = async (userId: string, planId: string) => {
    const [row] = await ctx.dataSource.query(
      `INSERT INTO purchases (user_id, kind, plan_id, amount, method, status) VALUES ($1, 'plan', $2, 100, 'cash', 'approved') RETURNING id`, [userId, planId]);
    return row.id as string;
  };

  it('activates idempotently and exposes /me/subscriptions', async () => {
    const { agent, userId } = await signUpClient(ctx.app);
    const plan = await planBySlug('performance-trimestral');
    const purchaseId = await purchase(userId, plan.id);
    const today = businessToday();
    const a = await ctx.dataSource.transaction((m) => subs.activateFromPurchase(m, { purchaseId, userId, plan, today }));
    const b = await ctx.dataSource.transaction((m) => subs.activateFromPurchase(m, { purchaseId, userId, plan, today }));
    expect(b.id).toBe(a.id);
    const res = await agent.get('/api/me/subscriptions').expect(200);
    expect(res.body.performance).toMatchObject({ planName: 'Plan Trimestral', startsOn: today, status: 'active' });
    expect(res.body.recoveryPacks).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('never oversells a pack under concurrency', async () => {
    const { userId } = await signUpClient(ctx.app);
    const plan = await planBySlug('recovery-paquete-4');
    const purchaseId = await purchase(userId, plan.id);
    const today = businessToday();
    const pack = await ctx.dataSource.transaction((m) => subs.activateFromPurchase(m, { purchaseId, userId, plan, today }));
    await ctx.dataSource.query(`UPDATE subscriptions SET sessions_used = 3 WHERE id = $1`, [pack.id]);
    const date = addDays(today, 1);
    const results = await Promise.all([
      ctx.dataSource.transaction((m) => subs.consumePackSession(m, userId, date)),
      ctx.dataSource.transaction((m) => subs.consumePackSession(m, userId, date)),
    ]);
    expect(results.filter(Boolean)).toHaveLength(1);
    await ctx.dataSource.transaction((m) => subs.releasePackSession(m, pack.id));
    const [row] = await ctx.dataSource.query(`SELECT sessions_used FROM subscriptions WHERE id = $1`, [pack.id]);
    expect(row.sessions_used).toBe(3);
  });

  it('covers the last day of the period', async () => {
    const { userId } = await signUpClient(ctx.app);
    const plan = await planBySlug('performance-mensual');
    await ctx.dataSource.query(
      `INSERT INTO subscriptions (user_id, plan_id, service, starts_on, ends_on) VALUES ($1, $2, 'performance', '2026-10-15', '2026-11-15')`, [userId, plan.id]);
    const yes = await ctx.dataSource.transaction((m) => subs.findCoveringPerformance(m, userId, '2026-11-15'));
    const no = await ctx.dataSource.transaction((m) => subs.findCoveringPerformance(m, userId, '2026-11-16'));
    expect(yes).not.toBeNull();
    expect(no).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

    - `listActivityForUser(userId: string): Promise<ActivityItemDto[]>` — ítems `attendance` (turnos con asistencia), `cancellation` (reservas canceladas) y `booking` (reservas confirmadas futuras), con las etiquetas del Step 5.

  - `SlotStatsService`: `listPublicSlots(service, date, viewerId?): Promise<TimeSlotDto[]>`, `availability(service, from, days): Promise<AvailabilityDayDto[]>`, `listStaffSlots(date: string, service?: Service): Promise<StaffTimeSlotDto[]>`, `pendingAttendanceSlots(today: string): Promise<StaffTimeSlotDto[]>`, `countSlotsOn(date: string): Promise<number>`.
  - `AttendanceService`: `mark(bookingId, status, staffId): Promise<RosterItemDto>` (R9), `roster(slotId, q: PaginationQueryDto): Promise<Paginated<RosterItemDto>>`, `countPresentOn(date: string): Promise<number>`.
  - `ActivityMetricsService`: `getMetrics(userIds: string[], today: string): Promise<Map<string, UserMetrics>>` con `UserMetrics = ClientMetricsDto & { recentFrequency: number; baselineFrequency: number; usualFrequencyPerWeek: number }` (R10, ventanas desde settings); `getMonthlyStats(userId, today): Promise<MyStatsDto>`.
  - Funciones puras: `canClientCancel(input: { status; source; startsAt: Date; now: Date; cancelLimitHours: number }): boolean`, `bookingLabel(service, source, durationMinutes): string` (R15), `isBookable(slot, now, today, bookingWindowDays): boolean`, `computeFrequencies(presentDates: string[], today: string, recentWeeks: number, baselineWeeks: number): { frequencyPerWeek; recentFrequency; baselineFrequency }`.
  - Job `expire-holds`: `@Cron('* * * * *', { name: 'expire-holds' })` con `JobRunner.run('expire-holds', …)`.
  - Fixtures de test (`test/utils/fixtures.ts`): `createSlot(ds, { service, startsAt: Date, capacity, professionalId? }): Promise<string>`, `createProfessional(ds, name?): Promise<string>`, `givePerformancePlan(ds, userId, { startsOn, endsOn }): Promise<string>`, `givePack(ds, userId, { startsOn, endsOn, total, used? }): Promise<string>`, `planIdBySlug(ds, slug): Promise<string>` (todo con SQL directo; requieren el seed).
  - Endpoints de reservas y asistencia del HTTP contract (owner T-013).

**Acceptance criteria:**
1. R6 completo, con los códigos de error en el orden indicado; Performance sin plan que cubra la fecha del turno → 403 `PLAN_REQUIRED`; Recovery sin paquete → 409 `PACKAGE_REQUIRED`; Rehab sin habilitación → 403 `REHAB_FOLLOWUP_NOT_ENABLED`.
2. **Review Focus 1:** con un turno de cupo 14 y 13 reservas, dos reservas simultáneas → exactamente una 201 y una 409 `SLOT_FULL`; el turno queda en 14/14.
3. **Review Focus 3:** plan que vence el 15/11 → se puede reservar el turno del 15/11 a las 21:00 locales y no el del 16/11 a las 07:00.
4. R7: el cliente cancela dentro del plazo (el cupo vuelve a estar disponible y el paquete recupera la sesión); fuera de plazo → `CANCEL_WINDOW_CLOSED`; reserva paga → `BOOKING_NOT_CANCELLABLE`; **Review Focus 4:** reserva de otro cliente → 404 `BOOKING_NOT_FOUND`.
5. R8: una retención vencida pasa a `expired` con `expireHolds` y libera el cupo; `confirmPaid` sobre una retención vencida confirma si hay lugar y lanza `SLOT_FULL` si no.
6. R9: asistencia `present`/`absent`/`no_show` solo para reservas confirmadas de turnos de hoy o anteriores; se puede corregir; el roster lista activas primero y canceladas con `cancelledAt`.
7. `GET /schedule/time-slots` y `/schedule/availability` son públicos, cuentan solo reservas activas y marcan `bookedByMe` con sesión.
8. Los emails `booking-confirmed` (reserva) y `booking-cancelled` (cancelación por staff) quedan en `email_logs` con su clave.

```ts
import { bookingLabel, canClientCancel, isBookable } from './booking-rules.js';

describe('booking rules', () => {
  const startsAt = new Date('2026-10-14T21:00:00.000Z'); // 18:00 local
  it('lets clients cancel plan, package and followup bookings until the limit', () => {
    const base = { status: 'confirmed' as const, startsAt, cancelLimitHours: 2 };
    expect(canClientCancel({ ...base, source: 'plan', now: new Date('2026-10-14T19:00:00.000Z') })).toBe(true);
    expect(canClientCancel({ ...base, source: 'plan', now: new Date('2026-10-14T19:00:01.000Z') })).toBe(false);
    expect(canClientCancel({ ...base, source: 'payment', now: new Date('2026-10-13T00:00:00.000Z') })).toBe(false);
    expect(canClientCancel({ ...base, status: 'cancelled', source: 'plan', now: new Date('2026-10-13T00:00:00.000Z') })).toBe(false);
  });

  it('labels bookings', () => {
    expect(bookingLabel('performance', 'plan', 60)).toBe('Entrenamiento semi personalizado');
    expect(bookingLabel('recovery', 'package', 45)).toBe('Sesión Recovery · 45 min');
    expect(bookingLabel('rehab', 'payment', 60)).toBe('Evaluación y Diagnóstico · 60 min');
    expect(bookingLabel('rehab', 'followup', 60)).toBe('Seguimiento fisioterapéutico · 60 min');
  });

  it('checks the booking window in business dates', () => {
    const slot = { startsAt: new Date('2026-10-27T00:00:00.000Z'), cancelledAt: null }; // 26/10 21:00 local
    expect(isBookable(slot, new Date('2026-10-12T15:00:00.000Z'), '2026-10-12', 14)).toBe(true);
    expect(isBookable(slot, new Date('2026-10-12T15:00:00.000Z'), '2026-10-11', 14)).toBe(false);
    expect(isBookable({ ...slot, cancelledAt: new Date() }, new Date('2026-10-12T15:00:00.000Z'), '2026-10-12', 14)).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

`apps/api/src/modules/bookings/domain/metrics.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { computeFrequencies } from './metrics.js';

describe('computeFrequencies', () => {
  it('averages present days per week over the windows', () => {
    const today = '2026-10-12';
    // Ventanas (R10): reciente = [29/09, 12/10]; base = [04/08, 28/09]; frecuencia semanal = [15/09, 12/10] / 4.
    const recent = ['2026-10-12', '2026-10-09', '2026-10-06', '2026-10-01'];
    const baseline = Array.from({ length: 24 }, (_, i) => `2026-08-${String(5 + i).padStart(2, '0')}`); // 05/08 … 28/08
    const result = computeFrequencies([...recent, ...baseline], today, 2, 8);
    expect(result.recentFrequency).toBe(2);
    expect(result.baselineFrequency).toBe(3);
    expect(result.frequencyPerWeek).toBe(1);
  });
});
```

Las ventanas son: reciente `[addDays(today, −13), today]`, base `[addDays(today, −69), addDays(today, −14)]` y frecuencia semanal `[addDays(today, −27), today]`.

`apps/api/test/utils/fixtures.ts` según "Interfaces".

`apps/api/test/bookings.e2e-spec.ts`:

```ts
import request from 'supertest';
import { addDays, businessDateTime, businessToday } from '../src/common/time/business-time.js';
import { seedCatalogAndSchedule } from '../src/database/seed-data.js';
import { BookingsService } from '../src/modules/bookings/services/bookings.service.js';
import { createTestApp, type TestApp } from './utils/app.js';
import { signUpClient } from './utils/auth.js';
import { resetDb } from './utils/db.js';
import { createProfessional, createSlot, givePack, givePerformancePlan } from './utils/fixtures.js';

describe('bookings (e2e)', () => {
  let ctx: TestApp;
  beforeAll(async () => { ctx = await createTestApp(); });
  afterAll(async () => { await ctx.app.close(); });
  beforeEach(async () => { await resetDb(ctx.dataSource); await seedCatalogAndSchedule(ctx.dataSource); });

  const tomorrow = () => addDays(businessToday(), 1);

  it('requires a plan covering the slot date for Performance', async () => {
    const { agent, userId } = await signUpClient(ctx.app);
    const slotId = await createSlot(ctx.dataSource, { service: 'performance', startsAt: businessDateTime(tomorrow(), '19:00'), capacity: 14 });
    await agent.post('/api/bookings').send({ timeSlotId: slotId }).expect(403).expect((r) => expect(r.body.code).toBe('PLAN_REQUIRED'));
    await givePerformancePlan(ctx.dataSource, userId, { startsOn: businessToday(), endsOn: tomorrow() });
    const res = await agent.post('/api/bookings').send({ timeSlotId: slotId }).expect(201);
    expect(res.body).toMatchObject({ status: 'confirmed', source: 'plan', label: 'Entrenamiento semi personalizado', canCancel: true });
    await agent.post('/api/bookings').send({ timeSlotId: slotId }).expect(409).expect((r) => expect(r.body.code).toBe('ALREADY_BOOKED'));
    const [log] = await ctx.dataSource.query(`SELECT template FROM email_logs WHERE idempotency_key = $1`, [`booking-confirmed:${res.body.id}`]);
    expect(log.template).toBe('booking-confirmed');
  });

  it('evaluates validity on the local date of the slot (end of day)', async () => {
    const { agent, userId } = await signUpClient(ctx.app);
    const last = addDays(businessToday(), 3);
    await givePerformancePlan(ctx.dataSource, userId, { startsOn: businessToday(), endsOn: last });
    const lateOnLastDay = await createSlot(ctx.dataSource, { service: 'performance', startsAt: businessDateTime(last, '21:00'), capacity: 14 });
    const nextMorning = await createSlot(ctx.dataSource, { service: 'performance', startsAt: businessDateTime(addDays(last, 1), '07:00'), capacity: 14 });
    await agent.post('/api/bookings').send({ timeSlotId: lateOnLastDay }).expect(201);
    await agent.post('/api/bookings').send({ timeSlotId: nextMorning }).expect(403);
  });

  it('never oversells the last spot', async () => {
    const slotId = await createSlot(ctx.dataSource, { service: 'performance', startsAt: businessDateTime(tomorrow(), '19:00'), capacity: 14 });
    for (let i = 0; i < 13; i += 1) {
      const { userId } = await signUpClient(ctx.app);
      await ctx.dataSource.query(`INSERT INTO bookings (user_id, time_slot_id, status, source) VALUES ($1, $2, 'confirmed', 'plan')`, [userId, slotId]);
    }
    const a = await signUpClient(ctx.app);
    const b = await signUpClient(ctx.app);
    for (const u of [a, b]) await givePerformancePlan(ctx.dataSource, u.userId, { startsOn: businessToday(), endsOn: tomorrow() });
    const results = await Promise.all([
      a.agent.post('/api/bookings').send({ timeSlotId: slotId }),
      b.agent.post('/api/bookings').send({ timeSlotId: slotId }),
    ]);
    expect(results.map((r) => r.status).sort()).toEqual([201, 409]);
    expect(results.find((r) => r.status === 409)?.body.code).toBe('SLOT_FULL');
    const [{ count }] = await ctx.dataSource.query(`SELECT count(*)::int AS count FROM bookings WHERE time_slot_id = $1 AND status = 'confirmed'`, [slotId]);
    expect(count).toBe(14);
  });

  it('uses and returns Recovery pack sessions', async () => {
    const { agent, userId } = await signUpClient(ctx.app);
    const slotId = await createSlot(ctx.dataSource, { service: 'recovery', startsAt: businessDateTime(tomorrow(), '19:30'), capacity: 2 });
    await agent.post('/api/bookings').send({ timeSlotId: slotId }).expect(409).expect((r) => expect(r.body.code).toBe('PACKAGE_REQUIRED'));
    const packId = await givePack(ctx.dataSource, userId, { startsOn: businessToday(), endsOn: addDays(businessToday(), 29), total: 4 });
    const booking = await agent.post('/api/bookings').send({ timeSlotId: slotId }).expect(201);
    expect(booking.body.source).toBe('package');
    await agent.post(`/api/bookings/${booking.body.id}/cancel`).expect(200);
    const [pack] = await ctx.dataSource.query(`SELECT sessions_used FROM subscriptions WHERE id = $1`, [packId]);
    expect(pack.sessions_used).toBe(0);
  });

  it('requires follow-up enablement for Rehab', async () => {
    const { agent, userId } = await signUpClient(ctx.app);
    const pro = await createProfessional(ctx.dataSource);
    const slotId = await createSlot(ctx.dataSource, { service: 'rehab', startsAt: businessDateTime(tomorrow(), '11:00'), capacity: 1, professionalId: pro });
    await agent.post('/api/bookings').send({ timeSlotId: slotId }).expect(403);
    await ctx.dataSource.query(`UPDATE users SET rehab_followup_enabled = true WHERE id = $1`, [userId]);
    const res = await agent.post('/api/bookings').send({ timeSlotId: slotId }).expect(201);
    expect(res.body.label).toBe('Seguimiento fisioterapéutico · 60 min');
  });

  it('enforces the cancel window and ownership', async () => {
    const owner = await signUpClient(ctx.app);
    const other = await signUpClient(ctx.app);
    await givePerformancePlan(ctx.dataSource, owner.userId, { startsOn: businessToday(), endsOn: addDays(businessToday(), 30) });
    const soon = await createSlot(ctx.dataSource, { service: 'performance', startsAt: new Date(Date.now() + 60 * 60 * 1000), capacity: 14 });
    const booking = await owner.agent.post('/api/bookings').send({ timeSlotId: soon }).expect(201);
    expect(booking.body.canCancel).toBe(false);
    await other.agent.post(`/api/bookings/${booking.body.id}/cancel`).expect(404);
    await owner.agent.post(`/api/bookings/${booking.body.id}/cancel`).expect(409)
      .expect((r) => expect(r.body.code).toBe('CANCEL_WINDOW_CLOSED'));
  });

  it('expires abandoned holds and re-confirms only with room', async () => {
    const service = ctx.app.get(BookingsService);
    const a = await signUpClient(ctx.app);
    const b = await signUpClient(ctx.app);
    const slotId = await createSlot(ctx.dataSource, { service: 'recovery', startsAt: businessDateTime(tomorrow(), '18:30'), capacity: 1 });
    const hold = await ctx.dataSource.transaction((m) =>
      service.holdForPayment(m, { userId: a.userId, timeSlotId: slotId, expectedService: 'recovery', holdMinutes: 15 }));
    await ctx.dataSource.query(`UPDATE bookings SET hold_expires_at = now() - interval '1 minute' WHERE id = $1`, [hold.id]);
    expect(await service.expireHolds(new Date())).toBe(1);
    await givePack(ctx.dataSource, b.userId, { startsOn: businessToday(), endsOn: addDays(businessToday(), 29), total: 4 });
    await b.agent.post('/api/bookings').send({ timeSlotId: slotId }).expect(201);
    await expect(ctx.dataSource.transaction((m) => service.confirmPaid(m, hold.id))).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'SLOT_FULL' });
  });

  it('lists time slots publicly with counts', async () => {
    const slotId = await createSlot(ctx.dataSource, { service: 'performance', startsAt: businessDateTime(tomorrow(), '07:00'), capacity: 14 });
    const res = await request(ctx.app.getHttpServer()).get(`/api/schedule/time-slots?service=performance&date=${tomorrow()}`).expect(200);
    expect(res.body.find((s: { id: string }) => s.id === slotId)).toMatchObject({ capacity: 14, booked: 0, available: 14, isFull: false, bookedByMe: false });
  });
});
```

`apps/api/test/attendance.e2e-spec.ts`: staff marca `present`, luego corrige a `no_show` (una sola fila en `attendances`); marcar un turno de mañana → 409 `ATTENDANCE_NOT_ALLOWED`; roster de un turno con 2 activas y 1 cancelada lista primero las activas con `attendance` y al final la cancelada con `cancelledAt`; `GET /api/staff/time-slots?date=<hoy>` devuelve `present`, `absent`, `noShow`, `cancelled`, `pendingAttendance`; `pending-attendance` incluye un turno de ayer con una reserva sin marcar; `POST /api/staff/time-slots/:id/cancel` con reservas activas → 409 `SLOT_HAS_BOOKINGS`; `PATCH capacity` menor a las activas → 409 `CAPACITY_BELOW_BOOKED`; staff cancela una reserva → `booking-cancelled` en `email_logs`; cliente en `/api/staff/time-slots` → 403. Los turnos pasados se crean con `createSlot` y `startsAt` en el pasado.

`apps/api/test/availability.e2e-spec.ts`: `GET /api/schedule/availability?service=rehab&from=<hoy>&days=7` devuelve 7 ítems con `available` = suma de lugares libres de turnos futuros reservables de cada día; un turno cancelado no suma.

- [ ] **Step 4: Implementar services, controllers y job**

- `BookingsService.book`: `dataSource.transaction` con los pasos de R6 usando `ScheduleService.lockSlot(manager, …)`; el conteo de activas es `SELECT count(*) FROM bookings WHERE time_slot_id = $1 AND status IN ('pending_payment','confirmed') AND deleted_at IS NULL` **después** del lock. Si el índice único parcial salta (`23505` sobre `UQ_bookings_active_user_slot`) → `ALREADY_BOOKED`. Después del commit, `sendBookingConfirmation`.
- `sendBookingConfirmation`: carga usuario y turno; `NotificationsService.send({ template: 'booking-confirmed', idempotencyKey: `booking-confirmed:${id}`, params: { name, serviceName: SERVICE_LABEL, label, date: DD/MM/YYYY, time: HH:mm } })`.
- `cancelByStaff`: misma transacción que R7; después `booking-cancelled`.
- `SlotStatsService`: una sola query agregada por lista (`LEFT JOIN bookings … GROUP BY time_slots.id` con `count(*) FILTER (WHERE …)`), sin N+1.
- `AttendanceService.mark`: `INSERT … ON CONFLICT (booking_id) DO UPDATE SET status, marked_by, marked_at`.
- `ActivityMetricsService.getMetrics`: una query que trae, para los `userIds`, las fechas de negocio (`(starts_at AT TIME ZONE 'America/Montevideo')::date`) de asistencias `present` Performance de los últimos `(frequencyRecentWeeks + frequencyBaselineWeeks) × 7` días + la última asistencia histórica, y conteos de cancelaciones, no shows, Rehab y Recovery; luego `computeFrequencies` en memoria.
- Controllers con las rutas, auth y DTOs exactos del HTTP contract (`date` con regex `^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$`, `service` con `@IsIn`, `days` 1–31).
- `expire-holds.job.ts`: `@Cron('* * * * *', { name: 'expire-holds' })` → `jobRunner.run('expire-holds', async () => ({ expired: await bookings.expireHolds(new Date()) }))`.

- Create: `apps/web/src/components/booking/performance-schedule.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/booking/performance-schedule.test.tsx`
- Create: `apps/web/src/app/(public)/reservar/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/(cliente)/reservar/fisioterapia/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/booking/followup-booking.tsx`
- Modify: `apps/web/src/app/(public)/performance/page.tsx` (montar `PerformanceSchedule` en `#horarios`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `apiFetch`, `errorMessage`, `ApiError`, tipos (`TimeSlotDto`, `AvailabilityDayDto`, `PublicSettingsDto`, `BookingDto`, `MeDto`), `formatTime`, `formatWeekdayShort`, `formatDayMonth`, `formatDateLong`, `businessTodayWeb`, `addDaysWeb` (T-003); `authClient.useSession` (T-003) para saber si hay sesión en componentes cliente.
- Produces (los usa T-019):
  - `DaySelector({ from: string; days: number; value: string; onChange(date) })` — `<select aria-label="Día">` con opciones "Lunes 12/10", como el mockup.
  - `WeekSelector({ weekStart: string; availability: AvailabilityDayDto[]; value: string | null; onChange(date); onPrevWeek(); onNextWeek(); minDate: string; maxDate: string })` — 7 columnas Lun–Dom; días sin disponibilidad deshabilitados.
  - `SlotGrid({ slots: TimeSlotDto[]; onBook(slot); pendingSlotId?: string | null })` — tarjetas con hora, `booked / capacity cupos`, botón "Reservar" o estado "Completo" (opacidad 60, `bg-soft`, sin botón) o "Reservado" si `bookedByMe`.
  - `SlotList({ slots; value: string | null; onChange(slotId) })` — chips de horarios (selección única), tachados si `isFull`.
  - `PlanRequiredNotice({ renewHref })` — "Tu plan no está vigente para esa fecha. Renová para seguir reservando." + botón "Renovar plan".
  - `useBookSlot()` — mutation `POST /bookings`; ante `PLAN_REQUIRED` expone `planRequired = true`; ante `UNAUTHENTICATED` redirige a `/ingresar?next=<ruta actual>`; otros errores → toast `errorMessage`; éxito → toast "Reserva confirmada" e invalida `['time-slots']` y `['me','bookings']`.
  - `/reservar`: tres cards (Performance → `/performance#horarios`, Rehab → `/rehab#reservar`, Recovery → `/recovery#reservar`) + card "Fisioterapia — Seguimiento" → `/reservar/fisioterapia` solo si el usuario es cliente con `rehabFollowupEnabled` (lee `GET /me` en el cliente; sin sesión no se muestra).
  - `/reservar/fisioterapia`: `WeekSelector` + `SlotList` sobre turnos `rehab` + botón "Reservar"; si `rehabFollowupEnabled` es `false`, mensaje "El seguimiento fisioterapéutico se habilita después de tu evaluación." con link a `/rehab`.

**Acceptance criteria:**
1. `/performance#horarios` muestra "Horarios y cupos." con "Cupos en tiempo real", selector de día (hoy … hoy + `bookingWindowDays`) y la grilla del día con `n / capacity cupos`, como `03-performance.html`.
2. Reservar sin sesión lleva a `/ingresar?next=/performance`; con plan no vigente muestra `PlanRequiredNotice` con "Renovar plan" → `/planes?service=performance`; con éxito el turno pasa a "Reservado" y el contador se actualiza.
3. `SlotGrid` muestra "Completo" sin botón en turnos llenos.
4. Fisioterapia respeta la habilitación (D6).

```tsx
import { render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';

import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import type { TimeSlotDto } from '@/lib/api/types';
import { SlotGrid } from './slot-grid';

  id: 's', service: 'performance', startsAt: '2026-10-12T22:00:00.000Z', endsAt: '2026-10-12T23:00:00.000Z',

});

});
```

1. **Review Focus 2:** webhook con firma inválida o ausente → 401 `INVALID_SIGNATURE` sin tocar la base; con firma válida, el estado se lee de la API de Mercado Pago (nunca del cuerpo) y el mismo pago aprobado notificado dos veces —o por webhook y por conciliación— crea **una** suscripción y no extiende la vigencia dos veces.
2. Activación y aprobación ocurren en la misma transacción (R16): si `activateFromPurchase` falla, la compra sigue `pending`.
3. Monto distinto al de la compra → `needs_attention = true` sin efecto; pago aprobado tras retención vencida y sin cupo → `approved` + `needs_attention` (D23).
4. Checkout de plan requiere sesión de cliente y kind `period_plan`/`session_pack`; checkout de reserva acepta invitado (R17): email existente → 409 `LOGIN_REQUIRED`; sin sesión ni `guest` → 400 `GUEST_DATA_REQUIRED`; el invitado nuevo queda creado y recibe `account-invitation`.
5. Pagos rechazados liberan la retención; la conciliación expira compras de reserva cuya retención venció sin pago aprobado, y compras de plan pendientes por más de 24 h.
6. Staff registra pagos manuales que activan el plan en la misma transacción y envían `purchase-approved`; lista de pagos filtrable y paginada.
7. Emails `purchase-approved` y `booking-confirmed` se registran una sola vez por compra/reserva.

`apps/api/src/modules/payments/domain/webhook-signature.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { createHmac } from 'node:crypto';
import { verifyMercadoPagoSignature } from './webhook-signature.js';

const secret = 'test-secret';
const sign = (manifest: string) => createHmac('sha256', secret).update(manifest).digest('hex');

describe('verifyMercadoPagoSignature', () => {
  it('accepts the documented manifest', () => {
    const v1 = sign('id:123456;request-id:req-1;ts:1700000000;');
    expect(verifyMercadoPagoSignature({ signatureHeader: `ts=1700000000,v1=${v1}`, requestId: 'req-1', dataId: '123456', secret })).toBe(true);
  });

  it('lowercases alphanumeric ids', () => {
    const v1 = sign('id:abc123;request-id:req-1;ts:1;');
    expect(verifyMercadoPagoSignature({ signatureHeader: `ts=1,v1=${v1}`, requestId: 'req-1', dataId: 'ABC123', secret })).toBe(true);
  });

  it('rejects tampering and missing parts', () => {
    const v1 = sign('id:123456;request-id:req-1;ts:1700000000;');
    expect(verifyMercadoPagoSignature({ signatureHeader: `ts=1700000000,v1=${v1}`, requestId: 'req-1', dataId: '999', secret })).toBe(false);
    expect(verifyMercadoPagoSignature({ signatureHeader: undefined, requestId: 'req-1', dataId: '123456', secret })).toBe(false);
    expect(verifyMercadoPagoSignature({ signatureHeader: 'ts=1', requestId: 'req-1', dataId: '123456', secret })).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

Implementación:

```ts
import { createHmac, timingSafeEqual } from 'node:crypto';

export function verifyMercadoPagoSignature(input: { signatureHeader?: string; requestId?: string; dataId?: string; secret: string }): boolean {
  const { signatureHeader, requestId, dataId, secret } = input;
  if (!signatureHeader || !requestId || !dataId) return false;
  const parts = new Map(signatureHeader.split(',').map((p) => p.trim().split('=', 2) as [string, string]));
  const ts = parts.get('ts');
  const v1 = parts.get('v1');
  if (!ts || !v1) return false;
  const id = /^[a-z0-9]+$/i.test(dataId) ? dataId.toLowerCase() : dataId;
  const expected = createHmac('sha256', secret).update(`id:${id};request-id:${requestId};ts:${ts};`).digest('hex');
  return expected.length === v1.length && timingSafeEqual(Buffer.from(expected), Buffer.from(v1));
}
```

`apps/api/test/webhook.e2e-spec.ts`:

```ts
import { createHmac } from 'node:crypto';
import type { NestExpressApplication } from '@nestjs/platform-express';
import { Test } from '@nestjs/testing';
import request from 'supertest';
import { DataSource } from 'typeorm';
import { AppModule } from '../src/app.module.js';
import { configureApp } from '../src/app.setup.js';
import { businessToday } from '../src/common/time/business-time.js';
import { seedCatalogAndSchedule } from '../src/database/seed-data.js';
import type { ProviderPayment } from '../src/modules/payments/models/provider-payment.model.js';
import { MercadoPagoService } from '../src/modules/payments/services/mercado-pago.service.js';
import { PaymentsService } from '../src/modules/payments/services/payments.service.js';
import { signUpClient } from './utils/auth.js';
import { resetDb } from './utils/db.js';
import { planIdBySlug } from './utils/fixtures.js';

describe('Mercado Pago webhook (e2e)', () => {
  let app: NestExpressApplication;
  let ds: DataSource;
  const providerPayments = new Map<string, ProviderPayment>();
  const fake = {
    createPreference: vi.fn(async () => ({ id: 'pref-1', initPoint: 'https://mp.test/init' })),
    getPayment: vi.fn(async (id: string) => providerPayments.get(id)!),
    searchByExternalReference: vi.fn(async (ref: string) => [...providerPayments.values()].filter((p) => p.externalReference === ref)),
  };

  beforeAll(async () => {
    process.env.MP_ACCESS_TOKEN = 'TEST-token';
    process.env.MP_WEBHOOK_SECRET = 'test-secret';
    const moduleRef = await Test.createTestingModule({ imports: [AppModule] }).overrideProvider(MercadoPagoService).useValue(fake).compile();
    app = moduleRef.createNestApplication<NestExpressApplication>({ bodyParser: false });
    configureApp(app);
    await app.init();
    ds = app.get(DataSource);
  });
  afterAll(async () => { await app.close(); delete process.env.MP_ACCESS_TOKEN; delete process.env.MP_WEBHOOK_SECRET; });
  beforeEach(async () => { await resetDb(ds); await seedCatalogAndSchedule(ds); providerPayments.clear(); });

  const notify = (paymentId: string, secret = 'test-secret') => {
    const ts = String(Date.now());
    const v1 = createHmac('sha256', secret).update(`id:${paymentId};request-id:req-${paymentId};ts:${ts};`).digest('hex');
    return request(app.getHttpServer())
      .post(`/api/payments/webhook/mercadopago?data.id=${paymentId}&type=payment`)
      .set('x-signature', `ts=${ts},v1=${v1}`).set('x-request-id', `req-${paymentId}`)
      .send({ type: 'payment', data: { id: paymentId }, action: 'payment.updated' });
  };

  const buyQuarterly = async () => {
    const { agent } = await signUpClient(app);
    const planId = await planIdBySlug(ds, 'performance-trimestral');
    const res = await agent.post('/api/checkout/plan').send({ planId }).expect(201);
    return { agent, ref: res.body.externalReference as string };
  };

  it('rejects forged notifications', async () => {
    await buyQuarterly();
    await notify('111', 'wrong-secret').expect(401).expect((r) => expect(r.body.code).toBe('INVALID_SIGNATURE'));
    expect(fake.getPayment).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('activates exactly once for duplicated notifications and reconciliation', async () => {
    const { agent, ref } = await buyQuarterly();
    providerPayments.set('222', { id: '222', status: 'approved', statusDetail: 'accredited', externalReference: ref, transactionAmount: 10500 });
    await notify('222').expect(200);
    await notify('222').expect(200);
    await app.get(PaymentsService).reconcilePending(new Date(Date.now() + 60 * 60 * 1000));
    const [{ count }] = await ds.query(`SELECT count(*)::int AS count FROM subscriptions`);
    expect(count).toBe(1);
    const subs = await agent.get('/api/me/subscriptions').expect(200);
    expect(subs.body.performance.startsOn).toBe(businessToday());
    const [{ count: emails }] = await ds.query(`SELECT count(*)::int AS count FROM email_logs WHERE template = 'purchase-approved'`);
    expect(emails).toBe(1);
  });

  it('flags amount mismatches without activating', async () => {
    const { ref } = await buyQuarterly();
    providerPayments.set('333', { id: '333', status: 'approved', statusDetail: 'accredited', externalReference: ref, transactionAmount: 1 });
    await notify('333').expect(200);
    const [purchase] = await ds.query(`SELECT status, needs_attention FROM purchases WHERE external_reference = $1`, [ref]);
    expect(purchase).toEqual({ status: 'pending', needs_attention: true });
    const [{ count }] = await ds.query(`SELECT count(*)::int AS count FROM subscriptions`);
    expect(count).toBe(0);
  });
});
```

`apps/api/test/checkout.e2e-spec.ts` (simulador activo: sin `MP_ACCESS_TOKEN`, con `createTestApp`):
- cliente hace `POST /api/checkout/plan` con el Mensual → 201, `initPoint` contiene `/checkout/simulado?ref=`; `POST /api/payments/dev/simulate { externalReference, outcome: 'approved' }` → `status: 'approved'` y `GET /api/me/subscriptions` muestra el plan;
- checkout de plan con un `single_session` → 400 `PLAN_KIND_INVALID`;
- invitado sin sesión hace `POST /api/checkout/booking` con `rehab-evaluacion` y un turno rehab (fixtures) + `guest { name, email, phone }` → 201 con `bookingId` y `holdExpiresAt`; existe el usuario, hay `account-invitation` en `email_logs`, la reserva está `pending_payment`; simular `approved` → reserva `confirmed`, `booking-confirmed` y `purchase-approved` registrados;
- mismo email otra vez como invitado → 409 `LOGIN_REQUIRED`; sin sesión y sin `guest` → 400 `GUEST_DATA_REQUIRED`;
- `evaluation` con turno performance → 400 `SERVICE_MISMATCH`;
- simular `rejected` → compra `rejected` y reserva `cancelled` (el cupo vuelve a estar libre);
- `GET /api/checkout/purchases/:ref` devuelve `PurchaseStatusDto` sin sesión.

`apps/api/test/manual-payments.e2e-spec.ts`: staff registra efectivo por el Trimestral para un cliente → 201 `StaffPaymentDto` (`method: 'cash'`, `status: 'approved'`), el cliente queda con suscripción; `GET /api/staff/payments?method=cash` lo lista; cliente en `/api/staff/payments` → 403; `planId` de una evaluación → 400 `PLAN_KIND_INVALID`.

- [ ] **Step 4: Implementar**

- `applyProviderPayment` (R16) dentro de `dataSource.transaction`: `SELECT … FROM purchases WHERE external_reference = $1 FOR UPDATE`; upsert `payments` por `mp_payment_id` (`ON CONFLICT (mp_payment_id) DO UPDATE SET status, status_detail, amount, updated_at`); transición con `UPDATE … WHERE status IN ('pending','expired') RETURNING`; efecto con `activateFromPurchase(manager, { purchaseId, userId, plan: snapshot, today: businessToday() })` o `confirmPaid(manager, bookingId)` (capturar `SLOT_FULL` → `needs_attention = true`, sin relanzar); `applied_at = now()`. Después del commit: emails con sus claves (`booking-confirmed` vía `BookingsService.sendBookingConfirmation`).
- `webhook.controller.ts`: `@Public() @Post('payments/webhook/mercadopago') @HttpCode(200)`; lee `req.query['data.id']`, `x-signature`, `x-request-id`; si `!verify` → `AppError('INVALID_SIGNATURE')`; si `type !== 'payment'` → `{ received: true }`; si no, `getPayment(id)` y `applyProviderPayment`. Log solo del id del pago.
- `CheckoutService.checkoutBooking`: valida plan/kind/servicio; resuelve el usuario (R17); en una transacción `holdForPayment` + `INSERT purchases (kind 'booking', method 'mercadopago', amount = plan.price)`; después del commit, `createPreference` (o simulador) con `expiresAt = holdExpiresAt`; si Mercado Pago falla, en otra transacción compra `cancelled` + `releaseHold(…, 'cancelled')` y relanzar `PAYMENT_PROVIDER_ERROR`. Guarda `mp_preference_id`.
- `reconcilePending(now)`: compras `pending` `mercadopago` creadas antes de `now − reconcileAfterMinutes` y después de `now − 7 días`: si no es simulador, `searchByExternalReference` y `applyProviderPayment` por cada pago (el aprobado primero); luego, compras de reserva `pending` cuya reserva está `expired` → `expired`; compras de plan `pending` con más de 24 h → `expired`.
- `recordManualPayment`: una transacción con compra `approved` (`method`, `recorded_by`, `approved_at`, `note`), pago `approved` (`mp_payment_id` null, `applied_at`) y `activateFromPurchase`; después `purchase-approved`.
- Controllers con rutas, auth y DTOs exactos (`guest.email` con `@IsEmail()`, `phone` 6–20, `name` 2–80; `outcome` `@IsIn(['approved','rejected'])`).

- Produces:
  - `evaluateClientAlerts(input: { today: string; settings: SettingsValues; paused: { reasonId: string; recontactOn: string | null } | null; performance: { current: SubscriptionModel | null; upcomingAfterCurrent: boolean; latest: SubscriptionModel | null; hasLaterThanLatest: boolean }; metrics: { lastAttendanceOn: string | null; recentFrequency: number; baselineFrequency: number } }): AlertCandidate[]` con `AlertCandidate { type: AlertType; periodKey: string; context: Record<string, unknown> }` (R12, pura).
  - `computeCheckinFlag(input: { pain: PainLevel; goals: GoalsChange }): CheckinFlag` (R13, pura).

  - `ContactsService`: `create(alertId, staffId, dto): Promise<ContactDto>`, `listForUser(userId): Promise<ContactDto[]>`.

  - `CheckinsService`: `send(period: string): Promise<{ sent: number; period: string }>`, `pendingFor(userId): Promise<PendingCheckinDto | null>`, `answer(userId, checkinId, dto): Promise<{ flag: CheckinFlag }>`, `stats(period): Promise<CheckinStatsDto>`, `responses(q: { period: string; flag?: CheckinFlag }): Promise<CheckinResponseDto[]>`, `referRehab(checkinId, staffId): Promise<CheckinResponseDto>`, `lastAnsweredFor(userId): Promise<{ period: string; respondedAt: Date } | null>`.

**Acceptance criteria:**
1. R12 exacto, incluida la precedencia `prolonged_inactivity` > `no_attendance` y la exclusión de `frequency_drop` cuando hubo alerta de inactividad.
2. **Review Focus 5:** correr `detectAlerts` dos veces el mismo día crea las alertas una sola vez; un cliente en pausa no recibe ninguna alerta salvo `recontact` cuando llega `recontactOn`.
3. Registrar un motivo abre la pausa y marca como gestionadas las alertas abiertas de inactividad, frecuencia y vencimiento del cliente; una segunda pausa activa → 409 `PAUSE_ALREADY_ACTIVE`; cerrar sin pausa → 409 `NO_ACTIVE_PAUSE`.
4. Contactos quedan asociados a la alerta y al cliente con canal, resultado y staff; gestionar dos veces → 409 `ALERT_ALREADY_HANDLED`.
5. Check-ins (R13): envío idempotente por mes (manual dos veces no duplica filas ni emails), respuesta solo del dueño, `painDetail` obligatorio con molestia, alertas `checkin_pain`/`checkin_goals`, stats y "Derivar a Rehab" (habilita el seguimiento, gestiona la alerta, envía `rehab-referral`).
6. Los logs no contienen respuestas de check-in ni notas.

```ts
import { SETTINGS_DEFAULTS } from '../../settings/settings.defaults.js';
import { evaluateClientAlerts } from './alert-rules.js';

const sub = (over: Record<string, unknown> = {}) => ({
  id: 'sub1', userId: 'u', planId: 'p', planName: 'Plan Mensual', service: 'performance' as const,
  startsOn: '2026-09-18', endsOn: '2026-10-18', sessionsTotal: null, sessionsUsed: 0, purchaseId: null, createdAt: new Date(), ...over,
});
const base = {
  today: '2026-10-12', settings: SETTINGS_DEFAULTS, paused: null,
  performance: { current: sub(), upcomingAfterCurrent: false, latest: sub(), hasLaterThanLatest: false },
  metrics: { lastAttendanceOn: '2026-10-08', recentFrequency: 2.5, baselineFrequency: 2.5 },
};
const types = (input: Parameters<typeof evaluateClientAlerts>[0]) => evaluateClientAlerts(input).map((a) => `${a.type}:${a.periodKey}`);

describe('evaluateClientAlerts', () => {
  it('flags plans expiring within 7 days unless renewed', () => {
    expect(types(base)).toEqual(['expiring:sub:sub1']);
    expect(types({ ...base, performance: { ...base.performance, upcomingAfterCurrent: true } })).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('flags one week without attendance and prolonged inactivity exclusively', () => {
    const farEnd = sub({ endsOn: '2026-12-31' });
    const perf = { current: farEnd, upcomingAfterCurrent: false, latest: farEnd, hasLaterThanLatest: false };
    expect(types({ ...base, performance: perf, metrics: { ...base.metrics, lastAttendanceOn: '2026-10-03' } })).toEqual(['no_attendance:since:2026-10-03']);
    expect(types({ ...base, performance: perf, metrics: { ...base.metrics, lastAttendanceOn: '2026-09-15' } })).toEqual(['prolonged_inactivity:since:2026-09-15']);
  });

  it('flags frequency drops by month', () => {
    const farEnd = sub({ endsOn: '2026-12-31' });
    const perf = { current: farEnd, upcomingAfterCurrent: false, latest: farEnd, hasLaterThanLatest: false };
    expect(types({ ...base, performance: perf, metrics: { lastAttendanceOn: '2026-10-11', recentFrequency: 1, baselineFrequency: 3 } }))
      .toEqual(['frequency_drop:month:2026-10']);
  });

  it('flags recently expired plans without renewal', () => {
    const ended = sub({ startsOn: '2026-09-02', endsOn: '2026-10-02' });
    expect(types({ ...base, performance: { current: null, upcomingAfterCurrent: false, latest: ended, hasLaterThanLatest: false } }))
      .toEqual(['expired:sub:sub1']);
  });

  it('only raises recontact for paused clients', () => {
    expect(types({ ...base, paused: { reasonId: 'r1', recontactOn: '2026-11-01' } })).toEqual([]);
    expect(types({ ...base, today: '2026-11-01', paused: { reasonId: 'r1', recontactOn: '2026-11-01' } })).toEqual(['recontact:reason:r1']);
  });
});
```

`apps/api/test/checkins.e2e-spec.ts`:

```ts
import { addDays, businessMonth, businessToday } from '../src/common/time/business-time.js';
import { seedCatalogAndSchedule } from '../src/database/seed-data.js';
import { createTestApp, type TestApp } from './utils/app.js';
import { signUpClient, signUpStaff } from './utils/auth.js';
import { resetDb } from './utils/db.js';
import { givePerformancePlan } from './utils/fixtures.js';

describe('check-ins (e2e)', () => {
  let ctx: TestApp;
  beforeAll(async () => { ctx = await createTestApp(); });
  afterAll(async () => { await ctx.app.close(); });
  beforeEach(async () => { await resetDb(ctx.dataSource); await seedCatalogAndSchedule(ctx.dataSource); });

  it('sends once per month, answers with pain and refers to Rehab', async () => {
    const staff = await signUpStaff(ctx.app, ctx.dataSource);
    const client = await signUpClient(ctx.app);
    const noPlan = await signUpClient(ctx.app);
    await givePerformancePlan(ctx.dataSource, client.userId, { startsOn: businessToday(), endsOn: addDays(businessToday(), 30) });

    const first = await staff.agent.post('/api/staff/checkins/send').expect(201);
    expect(first.body).toEqual({ sent: 1, period: businessMonth(businessToday()) });
    expect((await staff.agent.post('/api/staff/checkins/send').expect(201)).body.sent).toBe(0);
    expect((await noPlan.agent.get('/api/me/checkins/pending').expect(200)).body.checkin).toBeNull();

    const pending = (await client.agent.get('/api/me/checkins/pending').expect(200)).body.checkin;
    await client.agent.post(`/api/me/checkins/${pending.id}`).send({ feeling: 'good', pain: 'mild', goals: 'same' }).expect(400);
    const answered = await client.agent.post(`/api/me/checkins/${pending.id}`)
      .send({ feeling: 'good', pain: 'mild', painDetail: 'Molestia en hombro izquierdo al hacer press', goals: 'same' }).expect(201);
    expect(answered.body).toEqual({ flag: 'pain' });
    await client.agent.post(`/api/me/checkins/${pending.id}`).send({ feeling: 'good', pain: 'none', goals: 'same' })
      .expect(409).expect((r) => expect(r.body.code).toBe('CHECKIN_ALREADY_ANSWERED'));

    const stats = await staff.agent.get('/api/staff/checkins/stats').expect(200);
    expect(stats.body).toMatchObject({ sent: 1, answered: 1, needsIntervention: 1 });
    const referred = await staff.agent.post(`/api/staff/checkins/${pending.id}/refer-rehab`).expect(201);
    expect(referred.body.alertStatus).toBe('handled');
    expect((await client.agent.get('/api/me').expect(200)).body.rehabFollowupEnabled).toBe(true);
    const templates = (await ctx.dataSource.query(`SELECT template FROM email_logs ORDER BY created_at`)).map((r: { template: string }) => r.template);
    expect(templates).toEqual(['checkin-invite', 'rehab-referral']);
  });
});
```

- `detectAlerts(today)`: carga clientes, pausas activas, suscripciones Performance y métricas en lote (sin N+1); por cliente arma el input de `evaluateClientAlerts`; inserta con `ON CONFLICT (user_id, type, period_key) DO NOTHING` y cuenta las insertadas por tipo.
- `list`: `AlertDto` con datos del cliente (`initials` de R15), plan actual (`planName`, `endsOn`), métricas (`lastAttendanceOn`, `frequencyPerWeek`, `usualFrequencyPerWeek`), `contactsCount`, `pause` y `checkin` (si `type` es de check-in).
- `InactivityService.start`: en una transacción inserta el motivo y `UPDATE followup_alerts SET status = 'handled', handled_at = now(), handled_by = $staff WHERE user_id = $1 AND status = 'open' AND type IN ('no_attendance','prolonged_inactivity','frequency_drop','expiring','expired')`; el índice único parcial traduce `23505` a `PAUSE_ALREADY_ACTIVE`.
- `CheckinsService.send(period)`: elegibles según R13; `INSERT … ON CONFLICT (user_id, period) DO NOTHING RETURNING id`; emails `checkin-invite` (`url = ${WEB_URL}/check-in`) solo para las filas insertadas.
- `answer`: valida dueño y estado; guarda respuestas, `flag`, `responded_at`, `status = 'answered'`, y crea alertas con `createCheckinAlerts` en la misma transacción.
- `referRehab`: `setRehabFollowupEnabled(userId, true, manager)`, gestiona la alerta `checkin_pain` del check-in y, después del commit, `rehab-referral` (`url = ${WEB_URL}/reservar/fisioterapia`).
- DTOs: `reason` `@IsIn` de los 6 códigos; `recontactOn` regex de fecha + validación `>= businessToday()` en el service (→ `VALIDATION_FAILED` con `details`); `channel`/`outcome` `@IsIn`; respuestas de check-in `@IsIn` y `@MaxLength(500)`; `painDetail` obligatorio si `pain === 'mild'` (`@ValidateIf`).

**Model:** sonnet · **Depends on:** T-014 integrated · **Parallel with:** T-017, T-018, T-020 · **Contracts:** HTTP contract, Frontend route map · **Vault:** screens/Pantalla checkout de pago, screens/Pantalla Rehab, screens/Pantalla Recovery, constraints/Cuenta se crea durante la reserva
